```
def smooth_logsum_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
    I = c_vec_I(x,n)
    m=I.max()
    shift = alpha*(I-m)
    maxs=shift.max()
    z=shift-maxs
    e=np.exp(z)
    w=e/e.sum()
    val = m + (maxs + np.log(e.sum()))/alpha + mu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
    return val
def smooth_logsum_grad(x,n,alpha,mu, use_full_conv=True):
    I=c_vec_I(x,n)
    m=I.max()
    z=alpha*(I-m)
    z-=z.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    wc=e.sum()
    if wc==0: w = zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else w=e/wc
    # optional threshold and loop sparse
    nonzero = np.nonzero(w > 1e-16*max(w?) or maybe abs(w)>1e-16)[0]
    dx=2/n
    if len(nonzero) < min(100, ...): loop over nonzero
    else: conv
    G = F - S (times dx)
    G += 2*mu*(sum-target)*ones
```

Fast move implementation details:
```
def fast_pair_c(h,cvec,i,j,delta,n):
    newc = cvec.copy()
    ar = arange_n (global)
    s=1.0
    # i +delta
    idx = (n-1+i) - ar
    newc[idx] += delta*(1.0 - h[ar])
    idx = ar + (n-1-i)
    newc[idx] += -delta*h[ar]
    # j -delta: sign -1
    idx = (n-1+j) - ar
    newc[idx] += -delta*(1.0 - h[ar])
    idx = ar + (n-1-j)
    newc[idx] += delta*h[ar]
    p1 = n-1+i-j; p2=n-1+j-i
    d2=delta*delta
    if 0<=p1<L: newc[p1]+=d2
    if 0<=p2<L: newc[p2]+=d2
    return newc.max()*dx
```

def local_pair_search(h,n,deadline):
    h=project; cvec=exact_cvec(h,n); C=dx*cvec.max(); best_h...
    ar=np.arange(n)
    build left/right idx if n<=1200
    base_steps = [0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,5e-5,2e-5]
    # maybe scale by dx? h values dimensionless.
    for round in range(...):
       if time>deadline: break
       G = logsum_grad_from_cvec(h,cvec,n,alpha=5000) # sum units
       # also exact active weights? We can compute top weights from cvec: alpha high.
       # candidate list:
       # top G indices: sort abs G descending (but G near 0 at optimum)
       # Since G may be very small, top arbitrary. Include active impact: compute subgradient for max shifts maybe.
       # We'll compute grad for top active shifts (where cvec > max - tol) sum normalized. This gives exact directional derivative for active max.
       active = np.where(cvec >= cvec.max() - 1e-10*cvec.max() - 1e-12)[0]
       G_act = sum grad_shift for active / len? But grad_shift O(active*n). Active maybe many. Use full logsum with alpha high enough. Simpler.
       idx_top = np.argsort(-np.abs(G))[:top_vars]
       candidates = pairs among idx_top, plus random pairs count.
       improved=False
       for i,j in candidates:
          if time break
          max_allowed = min(...)
          if max_allowed<1e-12 continue
          # choose direction based on G (or active derivative)
          if G[i] < G[j]: sig=+1 (increase i, decrease j) else -1
          # try magnitudes fractions of max_allowed
          for f in [1,0.7,0.5,0.35,0.25,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
             d= f*max_allowed
             if d<1e-9: continue
             if sig>0: delta=d else -d
             newC = test_move(...)
             if newC < C - 1e-15:
                h[copied?]; cvec=newc? test_move should return newc too to avoid recompute?
                update improved=True; break
          if time break
       if not improved: maybe try random pairs with smaller/larger steps; or break after several.
```

`logsum_grad_from_cvec` can be sparse if weights nonzero small. Since α high, loop over active shifts with w>1e-12. For each shift, grad_shift vector length n. Active maybe 1-20. Good. Could just use loop over active always for local (not full conv) since sparse. Function:
```
def grad_logsum_c(x,cvec,n,alpha):
    m=cvec.max()
    z=alpha*(cvec-m); z-=z.max(); e=exp(z); w=e/e.sum()
    nonzero = np.where(w>1e-14)[0]
    G=zeros
    for p in nonzero: G += w[p]*grad_shift_p(x,n,p)
    return G
```
grad_shift_p for c units (no dx) pair formula. For p index, d=p-(n-1); use idx arrays. This loops active shifts. For local n=1000, active maybe 10 =>10k ops, fine.

`smooth_c_grad_hybrid`:
```
def smooth_c_grad(x,n,alpha,mu, use_nonzero_thr=1e-14):
    c = cvec_fast(x,n)
    m = c.max()
    z = alpha*(c-m); maxz=z.max(); e=np.exp(z-maxz); s=e.sum()
    if s ==0: w=zeros; w[np.argmax(c)]=1
    else: w=e/s
    # Determine nonzero: w > 1e-15 maybe. For loop if count<=max_loop.
    nz = np.where(w > max(1e-15, 0? absolute))[0]
    dxnot=1.0 # c units no dx
    if len(nz) <= min(500, (c.shape? use threshold based on n)): 
       G=zeros
       for p in nz:
          G += w[p]*grad_shift_p(x,n,p)
    else:
       Q=w.astype(float)
       F=conv_full(Q, 1-x)[n-1:2n-1]
       R=Q[::-1]
       S=conv_full(R, x)[n-1:2n-1]
       G=F-S
    G += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n/2)*np.ones(n)
    return G
```
Threshold: If alpha low, weights broad, len(nz) large; conv. If len <= 100 maybe loop. For n small, loop even 1000 shifts? 1000*100=100k vs conv 100k; either. Set `max_loop = 200` maybe. But if len 300 and n=500, conv ~500k; loop 150k assignments but Python loop 300 vector ops; comparable. Could use conv if len > 100. Use `if len(nz) <= 80`. For low alpha, broad. For high alpha, few. Good.
But weights may have many tiny 1e-20; threshold 1e-14. If len >80 due to plateau (many equal max), loop too many; conv. However conv with dense Q maybe accurate. Good.
`grad_shift_p` function: for p (0..L-1), d=p-(n-1). Use vectorized:
```
g=np.zeros(n)
if d>=0:
    i=np.arange(d,n); j=np.arange(0,n-d)
    g[i] += 1 - h[j]
    g[j] -= h[i]
else:
    dd=-d; i=np.arange(0,n-dd); j=np.arange(dd,n)
    g[i] += 1 - h[j]
    g[j] -= h[i]
return g
```
This returns gradient of c_d (sum units) wrt h. Check d=1: i=1,n-1; g[1]=1-h[0], g[0]-=h[1]. correct. For d=-1: i=0,n-dd; g[0]=1-h[1], g[1]-=h[0]. correct.
No dx.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=inf; best_n=0
    def consider(h,n): project, exact c, update best; return
    # initial candidates
    if initial available: consider(it,n0)
    else: consider(np.full(512,0.5),512)
    # maybe generate a few simple starts and evaluate (without optimization) to have fallback
    # Coarse phase if time >500:
       coarse_n=120
       starts: downsample best if best_n else constant, random binary, random continuous
       for each up to 4, run optimize_smooth_full time ~30s then local_pair_search 10s, consider.
    # Main phase on best (or parent) n. Determine n_main: if best_n? If best from coarse n=120, we can optimize at 250? Maybe choose n=500 from parent? Need preserve parent.
       h_main = if initial exists initial else best_h upsampled to 500? Use 500 (dx 0.004) for final squeeze.
       Consider initial at n=500 via interpolate from best/parent.
       Run optimize_smooth_full for time ~ budget*0.35
       Run local_pair_search for time ~ budget*0.25
       Run SLSQP polish
       Run local again
    # Higher res phase if time >300:
       n_high = min(1200, 2*best_n maybe 1000)
       h_high upsample best; consider; optimize_smooth_full limited; local_pair_search if n<=800; consider.
    # More random restarts with remaining time:

           create perturbed best (noise scale small) at n=500; short L-BFGS 20s; local 10s; consider.
    # final project exact best and return.
```

Project implementation:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5*n
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0,1)
    for _ in range(100):
        s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
        if diff>0:
           room=1-h
           tot=room.sum()
           if tot<1e-15: h=target/n*np.ones? break
           h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
           tot=h.sum()
           if tot<1e-15: break
           h += (diff/tot)*h
    # final exact adjust
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       # choose interior with largest slack
       interior=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if interior.any():
           idx=np.where(interior)[0]
           # distribute in chunks to not out of bounds
           for idx_i in idx:
              room = min(1-h[idx_i], h[idx_i]+target? Wait if diff positive add up to 1-h; if negative subtract up to h)
              step = min(diff, 1-h[idx_i]) if diff>0 else max(diff, -h[idx_i])
              h[idx_i]+=step; diff-=step
              if abs(diff)<1e-16: break
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
But final distribution loop over many if diff large; after proportional should be small. Could simpler: use proportional then if diff remains, adjust a few. Since we clip before, maybe sum target not exact due to float. Use while with room.

def fast_convolve(a,b):
    if fftconvolve is not None and len(a)*len(b) > 2_000_000:
        return fftconvolve(a,b,mode='full')
    return np.convolve(a,b,mode='full')
```

def cvec_fast(h,n):
    if n <= 800: # direct convolve maybe exact?
       return np.convolve(h, (1.0-h)[::-1], mode='full')
    else:
       return fast_convolve(h.astype(float), (1.0-h)[::-1].astype(float))
```

Logsum objective:
```
def logsum_value(c, alpha):
    if c.size ==0: return 0.0
    m = c.max()
    z = alpha*(c-m)
    mz = z.max()
    e = np.exp(z-mz)
    return float(m + (mz + np.log(e.sum()))/alpha)
```
If alpha <=0 return max. But use >0.

`smooth_c_objective(x,n,alpha,mu)`:
```
c = cvec_fast(x,n)
val = logsum_value(c, alpha)
diff = x.sum() - 0.5*n
if mu !=0: val += mu*diff*diff
return val
```

`grad_shift_c(x,n,p)`:
```
L=2*n-1? p index
d = p - (n-1)
g = np.zeros(n)
if d >=0:
    # i d..n-1, j 0..n-1-d
    if n-1-d >0:
       i = np.arange(d,n)
       j = np.arange(0,n-d)
       g[i] += 1.0 - x[j]
       g[j] -= x[i]
else:
    dd=-d
    if n-1-dd >0:
       i=np.arange(0,n-dd)
       j=np.arange(dd,n)
       g[i] += 1.0 - x[j]
       g[j] -= x[i]
return g
```
If d=n-1, n-d=1; works. If d=-(n-1), dd=n-1, n-dd=1.

`smooth_c_grad(x,n,alpha,mu)`:
```
c = cvec_fast(x,n)
m=c.max()
z=alpha*(c-m); mz=z.max(); e=np.exp(z-mz); s=e.sum()
if s==0:
   w=np.zeros(c.shape); w[np.argmax(c)]=1
else: w=e/s
# threshold
nz = np.where(w > 1e-16)[0]
# To decide loop, count weights > 1e-14 maybe
nz = np.where(w > 1e-14)[0]
if nz.size <= 64:
   G=np.zeros(n)
   for p in nz:
      G += w[p] * grad_shift_c(x,n,int(p))
else:
   Q = w
   # Convolve formula. Need Q may have tiny values; ok.
   one_minus = 1.0 - x
   F = fast_convolve(Q, one_minus)[n-1:2*n-1]
   S = fast_convolve(Q[::-1], x)[n-1:2*n-1]
   G = F - S
# If nz.size ==0 due to threshold, set max shift gradient
if nz.size ==0:
   G=grad_shift_c(x,n,int(np.argmax(c)))
else:
   # if conv, no penalty? Add penalty
if mu != 0:
   G += (2*mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n))*np.ones(n)
return G
```
But if nz not include all weights due to threshold 1e-14, loop uses truncated weights (sum <1). For gradient of logsum, weights should sum 1; omitting tiny fine. If use conv, includes all. For loop threshold, tiny omitted negligible. Good.

def run_lbfgs_smooth(h,n,deadline, configs=None, mu_base=1000):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
    h_best=h.copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    if configs is None:
       if n <= 250: configs=[(10,200,800),(50,500,800),(200,1000,1000),(1000,2000,1000),(5000,2000,1000)]
       elif n <= 700: configs=[(20,500,500),(100,1000,700),(500,2000,800),(2000,2000,1000),(10000,2000,1000)]
       else: configs=[(20,1000,250),(100,1000,350),(500,2000,400),(2000,2000,500)]
    for alpha,mu,maxfun in configs:
       if time.time() > deadline - 3: break
       # adjust maxfun by time? Hard.
       try:
          res = minimize(smooth_c_objective, h.copy(), jac=smooth_c_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxfun,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50})
          h_new=project_feasible(res.x,n)
          c_new=exact_c5(h_new,n)
          if c_new < best_c - 1e-15:

          # always continue from projected res? If improvement, h=h_new else maybe h=projected res/tmp? Use h_new to explore even if exact slightly worse? It may be closer flat. Use h_new.
          h=h_new
       except Exception:
          pass
    return h_best, best_c
```

If indices precomputed. For high n on-the-fly function separate? We can have `test_pair_move_general(h,cvec,i,j,delta,n)` that uses ar and computes idx arrays; slower but for n>cached fallback. Or local only for cached n. Let's implement both:
```
def test_pair_move(h,cvec,i,j,delta,left_idx,right_idx,ar):
    newc = cvec.copy()
    one_minus = 1.0 - h
    if delta == 0: return newc, float(cvec.max())
    newc[left_idx[i]] += delta * one_minus
    newc[right_idx[i]] -= delta * h
    newc[left_idx[j]] -= delta * one_minus
    newc[right_idx[j]] += delta * h
    p1 = (n-1)+(i-j); p2=(n-1)+(j-i)
    d2=delta*delta
    if 0 <= p1 < newc.size: newc[p1] += d2
    if 0 <= p2 < newc.size: newc[p2] += d2
    return newc, float(newc.max())
```
But this multiplies delta * one_minus array; if delta negative, fine. For `newc[left_idx[i]] += delta * one_minus`, left_idx[i] unique. `newc[right_idx[i]] -= delta * h`: if delta negative, adds. Correct from earlier signs for i +delta, j -delta (with signed delta). Check: i changes +delta, j changes -delta.
For i left: +delta*(1-h_jright). The array one_minus corresponds h_q where q=0..n-1; left_idx[i]=n-1+i-q, value 1-h[q]. Good.
For i right: -delta*h_p; right_idx[i]=n-1+i+p? Wait earlier right_idx for variable i: p=0..n-1 left variable? shift = p + (n-1-i), value h_p. `newc[right_idx[i]] -= delta*h[ar]` yes.
For j sign -delta: left contribution (-delta)*(1-h_q) => `- delta * one_minus` if delta positive? Actually if variable j sign s=-1, s*δ = -δ. Use code `newc[left_idx[j]] += s_delta * one_minus` with s_delta=-delta. If we pass delta (signed for i), then j sign = -delta. So `newc[left_idx[j]] += (-delta)*one_minus`, i.e., `-= delta*one_minus`. Good. j right: -s_delta*h = +delta*h. Good.
If delta negative, signs flip accordingly.

Second-order terms: p1 = n-1 + i-j (shift d=i-j), p2=n-1+j-i. Need add δ² (square of signed delta) to both. Because two terms contain both variables; regardless sign, change includes (+ (Δ_i)(Δ_j))? For term left=i,right=j: Δh_i=δ, Δh_j=-δ. Product change linear included, second order? (h_i+δ)(1-h_j+δ) (since -Δh_j = +δ? Wait h_j' = h_j - δ. 1-h_j' = 1-h_j + δ. Product expansion: h_i(1-h_j)+δ(1-h_j)+δ h_i + δ². Yes δ². For term left=j,right=i: h_j' -δ, 1-h_i' = 1-h_i-δ; product: old -δ(1-h_i) + δ h_j + δ². δ² positive. So add `delta*delta` to both shifts. Good.

But do we double count when d=i-j = j-i (i=j)? We skip i==j. For i≠j, p1 and p2 distinct. Add both. If i-j shift maybe out of range? i-j∈[-n+1,n-1], p valid. Good.

Now, advanced indexing: `newc[left_idx[j]] += ...` after `newc[left_idx[i]] += ...` may have overlapping indices; across calls accumulates correctly because each call no repeats? But within a call left_idx[i] no repeats. However, if i and j calls happen sequentially, second call uses advanced indexing `newc[idx] += vals`; if idx has no repeats, it will read current newc at those indices (which may have been modified by first) and add. Good.

Potential issue with numpy advanced indexing `+=` and overlapping within separate calls: It's safe because each `__setitem__` for advanced indexing evaluates RHS, gets current values for RHS, adds, and writes back. It does not have repeat within same operation. Overlaps across operations are not an issue. Good.

def local_pair_search(h,n,deadline, max_cand=80, random_pairs=400, scale_start=0.5):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cvec=exact_cvec(h,n)
    C=float(np.max(cvec)*2.0/n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=C; curr_c=C
    if n > 1200: return best_h,best_c # maybe skip or use general limited
    left_idx,right_idx,ar=build_index_cache(n)
    scale=scale_start
    fracs_base=[1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125]
    micro=[1e-4,5e-5,1e-5,5e-6,1e-6]
    L=2*n-1
    # Use alpha for gradient: higher as C? alpha_c=2000 maybe; c units scale n? α=2000 Δc 0.0025 ->5; good.
    alpha_g=2000
    no_improve_scan=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       G = gradient_from_cvec(h,cvec,n,alpha_g, max_loop=128)
       # top variables
       order=np.argsort(-np.abs(G))
       top=order[:min(max_cand,n)]
       # candidate pairs
       seen=set? Use loops.
       improved=False
       tested=0
       # top pairs
       for a_i in range(len(top)):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          i=int(top[a_i])
          for b_i in range(a_i+1,len(top)):
             if time.time()>deadline: break
             j=int(top[b_i])
             # allowed
             if G[i] < G[j]: sig=1
             elif G[i] > G[j]: sig=-1
             else: sig=1 if (i+j)%2==0 else -1
             # try plus/minus maybe both for top few
             signs = [sig]
             if a_i<10 and b_i<10: signs.append(-sig)
             for sgn in signs:
                if sgn>0:
                   maxd=min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
                else:
                   maxd=min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
                if maxd < 1e-12: continue
                # try fractions scaled
                vals=[]
                for f in fracs_base:

                # To avoid too many, unique

                best_newc=None; best_newC=curr_c; best_delta=0
                for dval in vals:
                   delta=sgn*dval
                   newc,newmax = test_pair_move(h,cvec,i,j,delta,left_idx,right_idx,ar)
                   newC = newmax*2.0/n
                   if newC < best_newC - 1e-14:
                      best_newC=newC; best_newc=newc; best_delta=delta
                if best_delta !=0:
                   h[i]+=best_delta; h[j]-=best_delta
                   cvec=best_newc
                   curr_c=best_newC
                   improved=True
                   if curr_c < best_c - 1e-15:
                      best_c=curr_c; best_h=h.copy()
                   tested+=1
                   if tested % 10 ==0:
                      # exact recompute
                      cvec=exact_cvec(h,n)
                      curr_c=float(np.max(cvec)*2.0/n)
                      if curr_c < best_c - 1e-15:
                         best_c=curr_c; best_h=h.copy()
                      # if drift made worse than fast, set curr
                # We break candidate after one accepted move; need recompute G? Maybe continue with updated G? Simpler break out to next scan? If we keep using old cvec for other candidates, accepted move invalidates old linear updates because cvec stored updated but G old; okay maybe. But to avoid long scan after improve, break to new scan.
               # if improved break top loops? We can use flag to break.
             if improved:
                break
          if improved:
             break
       # random pairs if time left and not too continuous? We can do after top if no break
       if not improved and time.time() < deadline - 5:
          # random pairs count maybe 300
          for _ in range(random_pairs):
             if time.time()>deadline: break
             i=int(rng global? Need random. In helper no rng? Use np.random.randint deterministic? Top-level function can use `np.random.default_rng`? It would create state each call? Let's detail `weights_from_cvec`:
```
def weights_from_cvec(c, alpha):
    m = c.max()
    z = alpha*(c-m)
    mz = z.max()
    e = np.exp(z-mz)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w=np.zeros(c.shape)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
        return w
    w=e/s
    return w
```

`candidate_deltas(maxd, scale)` top-level returns list of positive magnitudes.
```
def candidate_deltas(maxd, scale):
    vals=[]
    if maxd <= 1e-13: return vals
    base = maxd*scale
    for f in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
       v=base*f
       if v > 1e-13:
          vals.append(v)
    if maxd > 5e-3:
       for m in (1e-4,5e-5,1e-5):
          if m < maxd*0.98: vals.append(m)
    # unique within tolerance
    out=[]
    for v in vals:
       if all(abs(v-u)>max(1e-12,1e-4*v) for u in out): out.append(v)
    return out[:7]
```

Local search code without closure, need `rng` for random.
Pseudo:
```
def local_pair_search(h, n, deadline, seed=1, rng=None, top_k=70, random_pairs=300):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cvec=exact_cvec(h,n)
    curr_c=float(np.max(cvec)*2.0/n)
    best_c=curr_c; best_h=h.copy()
    if n < 2 or time.time() > deadline:
        return best_h, exact_c5(best_h,n)
    if n > 1500:
        # no index cache, maybe limited random with general test? skip
        return best_h, float(curr_c)
    if rng is None:
        rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    left_idx,right_idx,ar=build_index_cache(n)
    scale=0.4
    alpha_g=2000
    no_improve=0
    test_count=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        one_minus=1.0-h
        G=gradient_from_cvec(h,cvec,n,alpha_g,max_loop=128)
        order=np.argsort(-np.abs(G))
        top=order[:min(top_k,n)]
        improved=False
        # top pairs
        lt=len(top)
        for ai in range(lt):
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            i=int(top[ai])
            for bi in range(ai+1,lt):
                if time.time() >= deadline: break
                j=int(top[bi])
                # maybe if abs(G[i]-G[j]) very small and not top? still
                if G[i] < G[j]: sig=1
                elif G[i] > G[j]: sig=-1
                else: sig=1
                signs = (sig,)
                if ai < 12 and bi < 12:
                    if -sig != sig: signs=(sig, -sig)
                for sgn in signs:
                    if sgn > 0:
                        maxd=min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
                    else:
                        maxd=min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
                    if maxd <= 1e-13: continue
                    dvals=candidate_deltas(maxd, scale)
                    if not dvals: continue
                    best_delta=0.0; best_C=curr_c; best_cvec=None
                    for dv in dvals:
                        delta=sgn*dv
                        newc, newmax = test_pair_move(h, one_minus, cvec, i, j, delta, left_idx, right_idx)
                        newC = newmax * (2.0/n)
                        if newC < best_C - 1e-14:
                            best_C=newC; best_delta=delta; best_cvec=newc
                    if best_delta != 0.0:
                        h[i]+=best_delta; h[j]-=best_delta
                        cvec=best_cvec
                        curr_c=best_C
                        improved=True
                        if curr_c < best_c - 1e-15:
                            best_c=curr_c; best_h=h.copy()
                        break  # break sgn loop?
                if improved: break
            if improved: break
        # If improved, maybe exact recompute and continue; if no improve, random
        if improved:
            # exact recompute every accept maybe
            cvec=exact_cvec(h,n)
            curr_c=float(np.max(cvec)*2.0/n)
            if curr_c < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c=curr_c; best_h=h.copy()
            # reset scale maybe
            scale = min(0.5, max(scale, 0.2))
            no_improve=0
            continue
        # no improvement top
        # random pairs
        if time.time() < deadline - 2:
            for _ in range(random_pairs):
                if time.time()>=deadline: break
                i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
                if i==j: continue
                one_minus=1.0-h
                # use G sign if available
                if G[i] < G[j]: sig=1
                elif G[i] > G[j]: sig=-1
                else: sig=1 if (i^j)&1 else -1
                if sig>0: maxd=min(1-h[i], h[j])
                else: maxd=min(h[i], 1-h[j])
                if maxd <= 1e-13: continue
                # try a few
                dvals=candidate_deltas(maxd, max(0.1, scale))
                if not dvals: continue
                best_delta=0; best_C=curr_c; best_cvec=None
                for dv in dvals[:3]:
                   delta=sig*dv
                   newc,newmax=test_pair_move(...)
                   newC=...
                   if newC < best_C - 1e-14: ...
                if best_delta !=0: accept similar
        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            scale *= 0.5
            if no_improve >= 5 or scale < 1e-6:
                break
    # final exact
    cvec=exact_cvec(h,n)
    cu=float(np.max(cvec)*2.0/n)
    h_final=project_feasible(h,n)
    c_final=exact_c5(h_final,n)
    h_best=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    c_best=exact_c5(h_best,n)
    if c_final < c_best:
       return h_final,c_final
    return h_best,c_best
```

```
def grad_shift_c(x,n,p):
   g=np.zeros(n)
   d=p-(n-1)
   if d>=0:
      end=n-d
      g[d:n] += 1.0 - x[:end]
      g[:end] -= x[d:n]
   else:
      dd=-d; end=n-dd
      g[:end] += 1.0 - x[dd:n]
      g[dd:n] -= x[:end]
   return g
```

`project_feasible` with loops: Use `target=0.5*n`. For large n, room sum. If all h at bounds and diff nonzero, final distribution. We can implement robust but concise.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=0.5*float(n)
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
       s=h.sum()
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
          break
       if diff > 0:
          room=1.0-h
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot < 1e-15:
             h = np.clip(np.full(n, target/n),0,1)
             break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=s
          if tot < 1e-15:
             break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       # distribute
       if diff>0:
          order=np.argsort(1.0-h)
       else:
          order=np.argsort(h)
       for idx in order:
          idx=int(idx)
          if diff>0:
             step=min(diff, 1.0-h[idx])
             if step<=0: continue
          else:
             step=max(diff, -h[idx])
             if step>=0: continue
          h[idx]+=step
          diff-=step
          if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
If target/n >1? For n=1 target0.5; fine. If n=2 target1; h fill can be 0.5.

`interp_vector(src, n_src, n_dst)`: If n_src==n_dst return src.copy. Use np.linspace. We need avoid if n_src<2.
```
def interp_vector(src,n_src,n_dst):
    if n_src == n_dst: return np.array(src,dtype=float).copy()
    if n_src <=1: return np.full(n_dst, float(src.reshape(-1).mean()))? 
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_src)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_dst)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,src)
```

def make_start(n, mode, rng, parent=None, parent_n=0):
    if parent is not None and parent_n >= 2:
       base=interp_vector(parent,parent_n,n)
    else:
       base=np.full(n,0.5)
    base=np.clip(base,0,1)
    if mode == 0:
       h=base
    elif mode == 1:
       h=base + rng.normal(0,0.08,size=n)
    elif mode == 2:
       # binary
       h=np.zeros(n)
       k=int(round(n/2))
       idx=rng.choice(n, k, replace=False)
       h[idx]=1.0
       h=np.convolve(h, np.array([0.25,0.5,0.25]), mode='same')
    elif mode == 3:
       x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
       f=int(rng.integers(1,5)+1?) # 1..4
       phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35))
       h=base + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x/2?) Since domain length 2, frequency cycles over length 2: 2π f x /2? x in [0,2], sin(π f x). Use np.sin(np.pi*f*x+phase)
    else: maybe random plateau
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

Let's test fast update with n=2 manually using code signs. h=[a,b], cvec [a(1-b), a(1-a)+b(1-b), b(1-a)]. Let i=0,j=1, delta=+d: h0'=a+d,h1'=b-d. Left_idx[0] = [1,0], right_idx[0]=[1,2]? n=2: for i0 left_idx=[1,0], right_idx=[1,2]? Formula right_idx = ar+(n-1-i)= [1,2]? p: [1,2] yes. For i0:
newc[left_idx0] += d*one_minus: p1 += d*(1-b); p0 += d*(1-a).
newc[right_idx0] -= d*h: p1 -= d*a; p2 -= d*b.
For j1:

```
def run(...):
    global _DEADLINE
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5; _DEADLINE=deadline-2
    ... consider parent
    # Parent simple smooth if n<=800 and time>500
    if time.time() < deadline - 400 and best_n <= 800:
       t_end = min(time.time()+200, deadline-200)
       h,b = run_lbfgs_smooth(best_h,best_n,t_end, fast=False)
       consider
    # local on current best if time>300 and n<=1000
    if time.time() < deadline - 250 and best_n <= 1000:
       t_end = min(time.time()+150, deadline-120)
       h,b = local_pair_search(best_h,best_n,t_end, seed=seed+1, rng=rng)
       consider
    # pattern / alternate n if time>450
    if time.time() < deadline - 300:
       n_alt = 250
       # start from best
       h_alt=interp_vector(best_h,best_n,n_alt)
       h_alt=project_feasible(h_alt,n_alt)
       t_end=min(time+60, deadline-180)
       h_alt,_=run_lbfgs_smooth(h_alt,n_alt,t_end, fast=True)
       h_alt,_=local_pair_search(h_alt,n_alt,min(20, deadline-time-30), seed=seed+2, rng=rng)
       consider
       # two random pattern starts
       for mk in [3,1,2]:
          if time.time() >= deadline - 150: break
          h_start=make_start(n_alt, mk, rng, parent=best_h,parent_n=best_n)
          t_end=min(time+30, deadline-100)
          h_alt,_=run_lbfgs_smooth(h_start,n_alt,t_end, fast=True)
          h_alt,_=local_pair_search(h_start,n_alt,min(10, deadline-time-20), seed=seed+5+mk, rng=rng)
          consider
    # main 500 refine: use current best
    if time.time() < deadline - 220:
       n_main=500
       h_main=interp_vector(best_h,best_n,n_main)
       h_main=project_feasible(h_main,n_main)
       # if best_n==500 and already optimized, still run moderate
       t_end=min(time+180, deadline-120)
       h_main,b=run_lbfgs_smooth(h_main,n_main,t_end, fast=False)
       consider
       if time.time() < deadline - 120:
           t_end=min(time+160, deadline-80)
           h_main,b=local_pair_search(h_main,n_main,t_end, seed=seed+11, rng=rng)
           consider
       if time.time() < deadline - 80:
           t_end=min(time+50, deadline-60)
           h_sl=run_slqp_smooth(h_main,n_main,t_end,alpha=5000,maxiter=150)
           consider
           if time.time() < deadline - 40:
              t_end=min(time+40, deadline-25)
              h_main,b=local_pair_search(h_sl,n_main,t_end, seed=seed+17, rng=rng)
              consider
    # high res if time>160
    if time.time() < deadline - 150:
       n_high = 1000 if best_n < 1000 else 1200? cap 1200
       if best_n > n_high: n_high=best_n? Actually want higher than best? If best_n=500, 1000. If best_n=250, 1000. If best_n=1000, maybe 1500 but local skip.
       n_high = int(max(800, min(1200, 2*best_n))) if best_n < 1200 else 1200
       h_hi=interp_vector(best_h,best_n,n_high)
       t_end=min(time+120, deadline-80)
       h_hi,b=run_lbfgs_smooth(h_hi,n_high,t_end, fast=False)
       consider
       if time.time() < deadline - 60 and n_high <= 1000:
          t_end=min(time+60, deadline-40)
          h_hi,b=local_pair_search(h_hi,n_high,t_end, seed=seed+23, rng=rng)
          consider
    # remaining restarts
    restart=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 30 and restart < 6:
        restart +=1
        n_r = best_n if 200 <= best_n <= 800 else 500
        scale = 0.03 + 0.02*min(restart,3)
        h_r = np.array(best_h,dtype=float).copy()
        h_r += rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=n_r)
        h_r = project_feasible(h_r,n_r)
        if time.time() >= deadline - 25: break
        t_end = min(time.time() + min(50, max(15, (deadline-time.time())*0.4)), deadline-20)
        if time.time() < t_end-10:
            h_r,b=run_lbfgs_smooth(h_r,n_r,t_end, fast=True)
            best_h,best_c,best_n=consider_solution(h_r,n_r,...)
            if time.time() < deadline - 15:
               h_r,b=local_pair_search(h_r,n_r,min(25, max(5, deadline-time.time()-10)), seed=seed+50+restart, rng=rng)
               consider
    # final
    if best_h is None: ...
    final_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    final_c=exact_c5(final_h,best_n)
    # ensure not worse than parent? best tracks.
    return final_h, final_c, int(best_n)

`run_slqp_smooth`:
```
def run_slqp_smooth(h,n,deadline,alpha=5000,maxiter=100):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c0=exact_c5(h,n)
    if time.time() >= deadline-5: return h,c0
    _DEADLINE=deadline-3
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'jac':sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res=minimize(smooth_c_objective, h, jac=smooth_c_grad, args=(n,alpha,0.0), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'maxls':20}, callback=_time_callback)
       h2=project_feasible(res.x,n); c2=exact_c5(h2,n)
       if c2 < c0 - 1e-15: return h2,c2
       if c2 <= c0 + 1e-8: return h2,c2 # may not improve exact but smooth; consider best
       return h,c0
    except _StopOptimization: return h,c0
    except Exception: return h,c0
```
If SLSQP times out, res not; catch. Note if `_StopOptimization` is subclass Exception, except order: put _StopOptimization before Exception. Good.

def default_lbfgs_configs(n, fast=False):
    if fast:
       if n <= 250: return [(20.0,100.0,250),(100.0,300.0,350),(500.0,500.0,350)]
       if n <= 600: return [(20.0,200.0,150),(500.0,500.0,250)]
       if n <= 1000: return [(50.0,500.0,100),(1000.0,1000.0,150)]
       return [(100.0,1000.0,80),(1000.0,1000.0,100)]
    if n <= 200:
       return [(10.0,100.0,800),(50.0,200.0,1000),(200.0,500.0,1200),(1000.0,1000.0,1200),(5000.0,2000.0,1200)]
    if n <= 450:
       return [(20.0,200.0,500),(100.0,500.0,700),(500.0,1000.0,800),(2000.0,2000.0,1000),(10000.0,2000.0,1000)]
    if n <= 800:
       return [(20.0,500.0,300),(100.0,1000.0,400),(500.0,2000.0,500),(2000.0,2000.0,600)]
    if n <= 1200:
       return [(20.0,500.0,150),(200.0,1000.0,200),(1000.0,2000.0,250)]
    return [(50.0,1000.0,80),(500.0,1000.0,100)]

`interp_vector`: if n_src <=0, zeros? Return full average.
```
def interp_vector(src, n_src, n_dst):
    src=np.asarray(src,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n_dst=int(n_dst)
    if n_dst <=0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if src.size == 0: return np.full(n_dst,0.5)
    if src.size == n_dst: return src.copy()
    if src.size == 1: return np.full(n_dst, float(src[0]))
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,src.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_dst)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,src)
```

`make_start`: modes. For f integer: `f=int(rng.integers(1,5)) + 1?` `rng.integers(1,5)` gives 1-4. Good. For mode 3: phase, amp. Could also add random cos term? Simpler.
```
def make_start(n, mode, rng, parent=None, parent_n=0):
    if parent is not None and parent_n >= 2:
       base=interp_vector(parent,parent_n,n)
    else: base=np.full(n,0.5)
    h=base.copy()
    if mode == 1:
       h = h + rng.normal(0.0,0.10,size=n)
    elif mode == 2:
       h = np.zeros(n)
       k=int(round(0.5*n))
       if k>0 and k<n:
          idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False)
          h[idx]=1.0
       h=np.convolve(h, np.array([0.25,0.5,0.25], dtype=float), mode='same')
    elif mode == 3:
       x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
       f=int(rng.integers(1,5))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
       h = h + amp*np.sin(np.pi*f*x + ph)
       f2=int(rng.integers(2,6))
       amp2=float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.15))
       h = h + amp2*np.cos(np.pi*f2*x)
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    return h
```
Mode 0 returns parent projected. Good.

`consider_solution`: If h is None? no.
```
def consider_solution(h,n,best_h,best_c,best_n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n=int(n)
    if h.size != n:
       h=interp_vector(h,h.size,n)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=exact_c5(h,n)
    if best_h is None or c < best_c - 1e-15:
       return h.copy(), float(c), n
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

Now, local helper `build_index_cache`: We can compute using loops:
```
def build_index_cache(n):
    ar=np.arange(n,dtype=int)
    left=[]; right=[]
    for i in range(n):
        left.append((n-1+i)-ar)
        right.append(ar+(n-1-i))
    return left,right,ar
```
This allocates arrays. For n=1000, loop 1000, okay. Could be a bit slow but fine. To reduce memory, use `ar.astype(int)`. Good.

def test_pair_move(h, one_minus, cvec, i, j, delta, left_idx, right_idx):
    newc = cvec.copy()
    if delta == 0.0:
       return newc, float(cvec.max())
    newc[left_idx[i]] += delta * one_minus
    newc[right_idx[i]] -= delta * h
    newc[left_idx[j]] -= delta * one_minus

    L = newc.size

```

`candidate_deltas`:
```
def candidate_deltas(maxd, scale):
    if maxd <= 1e-13: return []
    vals=[]
    base=maxd*scale
    for f in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
       v=base*f
       if v > 1e-13: vals.append(v)
    if maxd > 2e-3:
       vals.append(1e-4)
       vals.append(1e-5)
    out=[]
    for v in vals:
       if v <= 1e-13: continue
       if v > maxd: continue
       dup=False
       for u in out:
          if abs(v-u) <= 1e-8*max(1.0, abs(u)): dup=True; break
       if not dup: out.append(v)
    return out[:5]
```
If base > maxd due to scale>1? We set scale<=0.5, but maybe; cap: `base = min(maxd, maxd*scale)`? scale positive, base<=maxd. If scale>1 maybe not. Add `v = min(v, maxd)`.

def run_lbfgs_smooth(h,n,deadline,configs=None,fast=False):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n); curr_h=best_h; curr_c=best_c
    bounds=...
    if configs is None: configs=default_lbfgs_configs(n, fast)
    for alpha,mu,mf in configs:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 2: break
        global _DEADLINE
        _DEADLINE = deadline - 3
        try:
           res = minimize(...)
           cand=project_feasible(res.x,n)
           c=exact_c5(cand,n)
           if c < best_c - 1e-15: best_c=c; best_h=cand.copy()
           if c <= curr_c + 1e-8:
               curr_h=cand; curr_c=c
           else:
               curr_h=best_h; curr_c=best_c # maybe best_c
        except _StopOptimization:
           pass
        except Exception:
           pass

    else:
        # maybe return best_h
        best_c=exact_c5(best_h,n); # ensure
        return best_h,best_c
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n); best_c=exact_c5(best_h,n)

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
except Exception:
    fftconvolve = None

_DEADLINE = 0.0


class _StopOptimization(Exception):
    pass


def _time_callback(_x):
    if _DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.time() > _DEADLINE - 3.0:
        raise _StopOptimization()
    return None


def fast_convolve(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    if fftconvolve is not None and a.size * b.size > 2000000:
        return fftconvolve(a, b, mode="full")
    return np.convolve(a, b, mode="full")


def exact_cvec(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size <= 1:
        return np.zeros(2 * h.size - 1 if h.size else 0, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def exact_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n),
                      np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size), h)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(exact_cvec(h, n)) * dx)


def cvec_fast(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    return fast_convolve(h[:n], (1.0 - h[:n])[::-1], "full")[:2 * n - 1]


def project_feasible(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size == 0:
            h = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            h = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n),
                          np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size), h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                h = np.clip(np.full(n, target / float(n)), 0.0, 1.0)
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if diff > 0.0:
                step = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
                if step <= 0.0:
                    continue
            else:
                step = max(diff, -h[idx])
                if step >= 0.0:
                    continue
            h[idx] += step
            diff -= step
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def interp_vector(src, n_src, n_dst):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n_dst = int(n_dst)
    if n_dst <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if src.size == 0:
        return np.full(n_dst, 0.5)
    if src.size == n_dst:
        return src.copy()
    if src.size == 1:
        return np.full(n_dst, float(src[0]))
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, src.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_dst)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, src)


def consider_solution(h, n, best_h, best_c, best_n):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size != n:
        h = interp_vector(h, h.size, n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = exact_c5(h, n)
    if best_h is None or c < float(best_c) - 1e-15:
        return h.copy(), float(c), n
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)


def logsum_value(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    mz = float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z - mz)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return float(m + (mz + np.log(s)) / alpha)


def smooth_c_objective(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    c = cvec_fast(x, int(n))
    val = logsum_value(c, alpha)
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * float(n))
        val += float(mu) * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def weights_from_cvec(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    m = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    mz = float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z - mz)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    w = e / s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            w = w / s
        else:
            w = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=float)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    return w


def grad_shift_c(x, n, p):
    n = int(n)
    p = int(p)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    d = p - (n - 1)
    if d >= 0:
        end = n - d
        g[d:n] += 1.0 - x[:end]
        g[:end] -= x[d:n]
    else:
        dd = -d
        end = n - dd
        g[:end] += 1.0 - x[dd:n]
        g[dd:n] -= x[:end]
    return g


def gradient_from_cvec(x, c, n, alpha, max_loop=512):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    w = weights_from_cvec(c, alpha)
    nz = np.where(w > 1e-14)[0]
    if nz.size == 0:
        G = grad_shift_c(x, n, int(np.argmax(c)))
    elif nz.size <= max_loop:
        G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for p in nz:
            G += float(w[int(p)]) * grad_shift_c(x, n, int(p))
    else:
        Q = w.astype(float)
        F = fast_convolve(Q, 1.0 - x, "full")
        S = fast_convolve(Q[::-1], x, "full")
        G = F[n - 1:2 * n - 1] - S[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
        G = np.nan_to_num(G, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return G


def smooth_c_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    c = cvec_fast(x, n)
    G = gradient_from_cvec(x, c, n, alpha, max_loop=512)
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * float(n))
        G = G + (2.0 * float(mu) * diff) * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
        G = np.nan_to_num(G, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return G


def default_lbfgs_configs(n, fast=False):
    n = int(n)
    if fast:
        if n <= 250:
            return [(20.0, 100.0, 250), (100.0, 300.0, 350), (500.0, 500.0, 350)]
        if n <= 600:
            return [(20.0, 200.0, 150), (500.0, 500.0, 250)]
        if n <= 1000:
            return [(50.0, 500.0, 100), (1000.0, 1000.0, 150)]
        return [(100.0, 1000.0, 80), (1000.0, 1000.0, 100)]

    if n <= 200:
        return [(10.0, 100.0, 800), (50.0, 200.0, 1000),
                (200.0, 500.0, 1200), (1000.0, 1000.0, 1200),
                (5000.0, 2000.0, 1200)]
    if n <= 450:
        return [(20.0, 200.0, 500), (100.0, 500.0, 700),
                (500.0, 1000.0, 800), (2000.0, 2000.0, 1000),
                (10000.0, 2000.0, 1000)]
    if n <= 800:
        return [(20.0, 500.0, 300), (100.0, 1000.0, 400),
                (500.0, 2000.0, 500), (2000.0, 2000.0, 600)]
    if n <= 1200:
        return [(20.0, 500.0, 150), (200.0, 1000.0, 200),
                (1000.0, 2000.0, 250)]
    return [(50.0, 1000.0, 80), (500.0, 1000.0, 100)]


def run_lbfgs_smooth(h, n, deadline, configs=None, fast=False):
    global _DEADLINE
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    curr_h = best_h.copy()
    curr_c = float(best_c)

    if configs is None:
        configs = default_lbfgs_configs(n, fast)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    for alpha, mu, maxfun in configs:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        _DEADLINE = deadline - 3.0
        maxfun = int(maxfun)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_c_objective,
                curr_h.copy(),
                jac=smooth_c_grad,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxfun,
                    "maxfun": maxfun + 10,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
                callback=_time_callback,
            )
            cand = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            c = exact_c5(cand, n)
            if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c
                best_h = cand.copy()
            if c <= curr_c + 1e-9:
                curr_h = cand
                curr_c = c
            else:
                curr_h = best_h.copy()
                curr_c = float(best_c)
        except _StopOptimization:
            pass
        except Exception:
            pass

    if curr_c < best_c - 1e-15:
        best_c = curr_c
        best_h = curr_h.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def sum_constraint(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(np.asarray(x, dtype=float)) - 0.5 * float(int(n)))


def sum_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(int(n), dtype=float)


def run_slqp_smooth(h, n, deadline, alpha=5000.0, maxiter=90):
    global _DEADLINE
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c0 = exact_c5(h, n)
    if time.time() >= deadline - 4.0:
        return h, float(c0)

    _DEADLINE = deadline - 3.0
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_constraint, "jac": sum_jac, "args": (n,)}]

    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_c_objective,
            h.copy(),
            jac=smooth_c_grad,
            args=(n, alpha, 0.0),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "maxls": 20,
            },
            callback=_time_callback,
        )
        h2 = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        c2 = exact_c5(h2, n)
        if c2 <= c0 + 1e-9:
            return h2, float(c2)
        return h, float(c0)
    except _StopOptimization:
        return h, float(c0)
    except Exception:
        return h, float(c0)


def build_index_cache(n):
    n = int(n)
    ar = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    left = []
    right = []
    for i in range(n):
        left.append((n - 1 + i) - ar)
        right.append(ar + (n - 1 - i))
    return left, right, ar


def test_pair_move(h, one_minus, cvec, i, j, delta, left_idx, right_idx):
    i = int(i)
    j = int(j)
    newc = cvec.copy()
    if delta == 0.0:
        return newc, float(cvec.max())

    newc[left_idx[i]] += delta * one_minus
    newc[right_idx[i]] -= delta * h
    newc[left_idx[j]] -= delta * one_minus
    newc[right_idx[j]] += delta * h

    L = int(newc.size)
    n = (L + 1) // 2
    mid = n - 1
    d2 = float(delta * delta)
    p1 = mid + i - j
    p2 = mid + j - i
    if 0 <= p1 < L:
        newc[p1] += d2
    if 0 <= p2 < L:
        newc[p2] += d2
    return newc, float(newc.max())


def candidate_deltas(maxd, scale):
    if maxd <= 1e-13:
        return []
    vals = []
    base = maxd * float(scale)
    for f in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
        v = base * f
        if v > maxd:
            v = maxd
        if v > 1e-13:
            vals.append(float(v))
    if maxd > 2e-3:
        vals.append(1e-4)
        vals.append(1e-5)

    out = []
    for v in vals:
        if v <= 1e-13 or v > maxd:
            continue
        dup = False
        for u in out:
            if abs(v - u) <= 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(u)):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            out.append(v)
    return out[:5]


def local_pair_search(h, n, deadline, seed=1, rng=None, top_k=None):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if n < 2 or time.time() >= deadline - 1.0:
        c = exact_c5(h, n)
        return h, float(c)

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    cvec = exact_cvec(h, n)
    curr_c = float(np.max(cvec) * (2.0 / float(n)))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(curr_c)

    if n > 1200:
        return h, float(curr_c)

    left_idx, right_idx, _ar = build_index_cache(n)
    top_k = int(top_k) if top_k is not None else (80 if n <= 300 else (60 if n <= 800 else 40))
    scale = 0.35
    no_improve = 0
    alpha_g = 500.0
    rand_count = 220 if n <= 500 else 140

    while time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        cvec = exact_cvec(h, n)
        curr_c = float(np.max(cvec) * (2.0 / float(n)))
        if curr_c < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = curr_c
            best_h = h.copy()

        one_minus = 1.0 - h
        G = gradient_from_cvec(h, cvec, n, alpha_g, max_loop=512)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
            G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

        order = np.argsort(-np.abs(G))
        top = order[:min(top_k, n)]
        lt = int(len(top))
        improved = False

        for ai in range(lt):
            if improved or time.time() >= deadline - 3.0:
                break
            i = int(top[ai])
            for bi in range(ai + 1, lt):
                if improved or time.time() >= deadline - 3.0:
                    break
                j = int(top[bi])
                if G[i] < G[j]:
                    sig = 1
                elif G[i] > G[j]:
                    sig = -1
                else:
                    sig = 1

                if ai < 14 and bi < 14:
                    signs = (sig, -sig)
                else:
                    signs = (sig,)

                for sgn in signs:
                    if sgn > 0:
                        maxd = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
                    else:
                        maxd = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
                    if maxd <= 1e-13:
                        continue

                    dvals = candidate_deltas(maxd, scale)
                    if not dvals:
                        continue

                    best_delta = 0.0
                    best_C = curr_c
                    best_newc = None
                    for dv in dvals:
                        delta = float(sgn) * float(dv)
                        newc, newmax = test_pair_move(
                            h, one_minus, cvec, i, j, delta, left_idx, right_idx
                        )
                        newC = float(newmax) * (2.0 / float(n))
                        if newC < best_C - 1e-13:
                            best_C = newC
                            best_delta = delta
                            best_newc = newc

                    if best_delta != 0.0:
                        h[i] += best_delta
                        h[j] -= best_delta
                        improved = True
                        break

                if improved:
                    break

            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            if scale < 0.18:
                scale = 0.18
            continue

        for _r in range(rand_count):
            if improved or time.time() >= deadline - 3.0:
                break
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if i == j:
                continue

            if G[i] < G[j]:
                sig = 1
            elif G[i] > G[j]:
                sig = -1
            else:
                sig = 1 if ((i + j) & 1) else -1

            if sig > 0:
                maxd = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
            else:
                maxd = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
            if maxd <= 1e-13:
                continue

            dvals = candidate_deltas(maxd, max(0.12, scale))
            if not dvals:
                continue

            best_delta = 0.0
            best_C = curr_c
            best_newc = None
            for dv in dvals[:3]:
                delta = float(sig) * float(dv)
                newc, newmax = test_pair_move(
                    h, one_minus, cvec, i, j, delta, left_idx, right_idx
                )
                newC = float(newmax) * (2.0 / float(n))
                if newC < best_C - 1e-13:
                    best_C = newC
                    best_delta = delta
                    best_newc = newc

            if best_delta != 0.0:
                h[i] += best_delta
                h[j] -= best_delta
                improved = True

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            if scale < 0.18:
                scale = 0.18
            continue

        no_improve += 1
        scale *= 0.5
        if no_improve >= 6 or scale < 1e-6:
            break

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c_curr = exact_c5(h, n)
    h_best = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c_best = exact_c5(h_best, n)
    if c_curr <= c_best:
        return h, float(c_curr)
    return h_best, float(c_best)


def make_start(n, mode, rng, parent=None, parent_n=0):
    n = int(n)
    if parent is not None and int(parent_n) >= 2:
        base = interp_vector(parent, int(parent_n), n)
    else:
        base = np.full(n, 0.5)
    base = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = base.copy()

    mode = int(mode)
    if mode == 1:
        h = h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, size=n)
    elif mode == 2:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        k = int(round(0.5 * n))
        if 0 < k < n:
            idx = rng.choice(n, k, replace=False)
            h[idx] = 1.0
        h = np.convolve(h, np.array([0.25, 0.5, 0.25], dtype=float), mode="same")
    elif mode == 3:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        f1 = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        f2 = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        amp2 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.15))
        h = h + amp1 * np.sin(np.pi * f1 * x + ph1)
        h = h + amp2 * np.cos(np.pi * f2 * x)

    return project_feasible(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    global _DEADLINE
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(20.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    _DEADLINE = deadline - 2.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = np.inf
    best_n = 0

    try:
        h_par = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_par = int(len(h_par))
    except Exception:
        n_par = 500
        h_par = np.full(n_par, 0.5)

    best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_par, n_par, best_h, best_c, best_n)
    if best_h is None:
        best_h = project_feasible(np.full(500, 0.5), 500)
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h, 500)
        best_n = 500

    # Initial benign refinement at the supplied resolution.
    if time.time() < deadline - 650 and best_n <= 800:
        t_end = min(time.time() + 120.0, deadline - 450.0)
        h_t, _c = run_lbfgs_smooth(best_h, best_n, t_end, fast=False)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_t, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    # Quick local pair polishing.
    if time.time() < deadline - 480 and best_n <= 1000:
        t_end = min(time.time() + 100.0, deadline - 330.0)
        h_t, _c = local_pair_search(best_h, best_n, t_end, seed=seed + 1, rng=rng)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_t, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    # Alternate-resolution search / escape from local basins.
    if time.time() < deadline - 430:
        n_alt = 250
        h_alt = interp_vector(best_h, best_n, n_alt)
        h_alt = project_feasible(h_alt, n_alt)
        t_end = min(time.time() + 50.0, deadline - 260.0)
        h_alt, _c = run_lbfgs_smooth(h_alt, n_alt, t_end, fast=True)
        t_end2 = min(time.time() + 20.0, deadline - 120.0)
        h_alt, _c = local_pair_search(h_alt, n_alt, t_end2, seed=seed + 2, rng=rng)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_alt, n_alt, best_h, best_c, best_n)

        for mk in (3, 1, 2):
            if time.time() >= deadline - 230:
                break
            h_s = make_start(n_alt, mk, rng, parent=best_h, parent_n=best_n)
            t_end = min(time.time() + 28.0, deadline - 150.0)
            h_alt, _c = run_lbfgs_smooth(h_s, n_alt, t_end, fast=True)
            t_end2 = min(time.time() + 10.0, deadline - 80.0)
            h_alt, _c = local_pair_search(h_alt, n_alt, t_end2, seed=seed + 10 + mk, rng=rng)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_alt, n_alt, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    # Main refinement at n=500.
    if time.time() < deadline - 310:
        n_main = 500
        h_main = interp_vector(best_h, best_n, n_main)
        h_main = project_feasible(h_main, n_main)

        t_end = min(time.time() + 120.0, deadline - 160.0)
        h_main, _c = run_lbfgs_smooth(h_main, n_main, t_end, fast=False)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_main, n_main, best_h, best_c, best_n)

        if time.time() < deadline - 180:
            t_end = min(time.time() + 110.0, deadline - 90.0)
            h_main, _c = local_pair_search(h_main, n_main, t_end, seed=seed + 20, rng=rng)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_main, n_main, best_h, best_c, best_n)

        if time.time() < deadline - 130:
            t_end = min(time.time() + 35.0, deadline - 70.0)
            h_sl = run_slqp_smooth(h_main, n_main, t_end, alpha=5000.0, maxiter=80)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_sl, n_main, best_h, best_c, best_n)

            if time.time() < deadline - 55:
                t_end = min(time.time() + 25.0, deadline - 30.0)
                h_main, _c = local_pair_search(h_sl, n_main, t_end, seed=seed + 30, rng=rng)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_main, n_main, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    # Higher-resolution trial.
    if time.time() < deadline - 160:
        if best_n < 1200:
            n_hi = int(max(800, min(1200, 2 * best_n)))
        else:
            n_hi = 1200
        h_hi = interp_vector(best_h, best_n, n_hi)
        h_hi = project_feasible(h_hi, n_hi)
        t_end = min(time.time() + 80.0, deadline - 70.0)
        h_hi, _c = run_lbfgs_smooth(h_hi, n_hi, t_end, fast=False)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_hi, n_hi, best_h, best_c, best_n)

        if time.time() < deadline - 65 and n_hi <= 1000:
            t_end = min(time.time() + 35.0, deadline - 35.0)
            h_hi, _c = local_pair_search(h_hi, n_hi, t_end, seed=seed + 40, rng=rng)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_hi, n_hi, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    # Randomized perturbations of the current best.
    restart = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 45 and restart < 5:
        restart += 1
        if 200 <= best_n <= 800:
            n_r = best_n
        else:
            n_r = 500
        h_r = interp_vector(best_h, best_n, n_r)
        scale = 0.018 + 0.012 * float(restart)
        h_r = h_r + rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=n_r)
        h_r = project_feasible(h_r, n_r)

        if time.time() >= deadline - 35:
            break
        t_end = min(time.time() + min(40.0, max(12.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.35)),
                    deadline - 25.0)
        if time.time() < t_end - 6.0:
            h_r, _c = run_lbfgs_smooth(h_r, n_r, t_end, fast=True)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_r, n_r, best_h, best_c, best_n)

            if time.time() < deadline - 18:
                t_end2 = min(time.time() + min(18.0, max(5.0, deadline - time.time() - 10.0)),
                             deadline - 12.0)
                h_r, _c = local_pair_search(h_r, n_r, t_end2, seed=seed + 500 + restart, rng=rng)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_solution(h_r, n_r, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = project_feasible(np.full(500, 0.5), 500)
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h, 500)
        best_n = 500

    final_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = exact_c5(final_h, best_n)
    return final_h, float(final_c), int(best_n)
```
